- [~] [L211](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/bitmap.h#L211) `class wxBitmap : public wxGDIObject {` — **Partial**: `drawing-canvas`, `make-drawing-canvas`, `canvas-content`, `canvas-client-size`, `save-canvas-image`, `bitmap`, `make-bitmap`, `bitmap-image`, `bitmap-logical-size`, `bitmap-dip-size`, `bitmap-scale-factor`; ABI: `wxl_drawing_canvas`, `wxl_set_canvas_content`, `wxl_canvas_client_size`, `wxl_save_canvas_image`, `wxl_bitmap`, `wxl_bitmap_image`, `wxl_bitmap_geometry`. Private retained drawing/value adapter via DRAWING-CANVAS/CANVAS-CONTENT and SAVE-CANVAS-IMAGE only. Bounded scene: opaque RGB bytes, signed DIP geometry, Swiss/sans point-size text and copied RGB bitmap buffers; private native DC/font/colour/image/bitmap resources never escape or become Lisp objects. Callback writes/export defer; source/ancestor/native-entry guards and staged scene replacement. Native paint exceptions are contained; PNG output uses private handler and checked non-atomic file write. No standalone class/API parity. See docs/DRAWING-CANVAS.md. Session-owned copied image/bitmap values, at most256 resources and64Mi total pixels,1..4096 pixel axes, GUI/main-thread checked. No raw buffers/adoption; writes/allocations/disposal defer during callbacks. See docs/IMAGES-DISPLAYS.md. Native conversion and independent value copies; immutable bitmap sizing, no raw adoption. ABI60 adds copied RGBA scene pixels with native alpha composition; same1..256 extent and2MiB aggregate cap.

ABI60 adds copied RGBA scene pixels with native alpha composition; same1..256 extent and2MiB aggregate cap.

Private retained drawing/value adapter via DRAWING-CANVAS/CANVAS-CONTENT and SAVE-CANVAS-IMAGE only. Bounded scene: opaque RGB bytes, signed DIP geometry, Swiss/sans point-size text and copied RGB bitmap buffers; private native DC/font/colour/image/bitmap resources never escape or become Lisp objects. Callback writes/export defer; source/ancestor/native-entry guards and staged scene replacement. Native paint exceptions are contained; PNG output uses private handler and checked non-atomic file write. No standalone class/API parity. See docs/DRAWING-CANVAS.md. ABI60 adds copied RGBA scene pixels with native alpha composition; same1..256 extent and2MiB aggregate cap.

- [~] [L313](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/font.h#L313) `class wxFontInfo {` — **Partial**: `drawing-canvas`, `make-drawing-canvas`, `canvas-content`, `canvas-client-size`, `save-canvas-image`; ABI: `wxl_drawing_canvas`, `wxl_set_canvas_content`, `wxl_canvas_client_size`, `wxl_save_canvas_image`. Private retained drawing/value adapter via DRAWING-CANVAS/CANVAS-CONTENT and SAVE-CANVAS-IMAGE only. Bounded scene: opaque RGB bytes, signed DIP geometry, Swiss/sans point-size text and copied RGB bitmap buffers; private native DC/font/colour/image/bitmap resources never escape or become Lisp objects. Callback writes/export defer; source/ancestor/native-entry guards and staged scene replacement. Native paint exceptions are contained; PNG output uses private handler and checked non-atomic file write. No standalone class/API parity. See docs/DRAWING-CANVAS.md. ABI60 adds copied RGBA scene pixels with native alpha composition; same1..256 extent and2MiB aggregate cap.

- [~] [L489](https://github.com/wxWidgets/wxWidgets/blob/v3.2.12/interface/wx/image.h#L489) `class wxImage : public wxObject {` — **Partial**: `drawing-canvas`, `make-drawing-canvas`, `canvas-content`, `canvas-client-size`, `save-canvas-image`, `image`, `make-image`, `image-rgb`, `image-alpha`, `image-pixel`, `scale-image`; ABI: `wxl_drawing_canvas`, `wxl_set_canvas_content`, `wxl_canvas_client_size`, `wxl_save_canvas_image`, `wxl_image`, `wxl_image_set_bytes`, `wxl_image_pixel`, `wxl_scale_image`. Private retained drawing/value adapter via DRAWING-CANVAS/CANVAS-CONTENT and SAVE-CANVAS-IMAGE only. Bounded scene: opaque RGB bytes, signed DIP geometry, Swiss/sans point-size text and copied RGB bitmap buffers; private native DC/font/colour/image/bitmap resources never escape or become Lisp objects. Callback writes/export defer; source/ancestor/native-entry guards and staged scene replacement. Native paint exceptions are contained; PNG output uses private handler and checked non-atomic file write. No standalone class/API parity. See docs/DRAWING-CANVAS.md. Session-owned copied image/bitmap values, at most256 resources and64Mi total pixels,1..4096 pixel axes, GUI/main-thread checked. No raw buffers/adoption; writes/allocations/disposal defer during callbacks. See docs/IMAGES-DISPLAYS.md. Explicit PNG operations use wxPNGHandler, not general image loading APIs. ABI60 adds copied RGBA scene pixels with native alpha composition; same1..256 extent and2MiB aggregate cap.

Private retained drawing/value adapter via DRAWING-CANVAS/CANVAS-CONTENT and SAVE-CANVAS-IMAGE only. Bounded scene: opaque RGB bytes, signed DIP geometry, Swiss/sans point-size text and copied RGB bitmap buffers; private native DC/font/colour/image/bitmap resources never escape or become Lisp objects. Callback writes/export defer; source/ancestor/native-entry guards and staged scene replacement. Native paint exceptions are contained; PNG output uses private handler and checked non-atomic file write. No standalone class/API parity. See docs/DRAWING-CANVAS.md. Session-owned copied image/bitmap values, at most256 resources and64Mi total pixels,1..4096 pixel axes, GUI/main-thread checked. No raw buffers/adoption; writes/allocations/disposal defer during callbacks. See docs/IMAGES-DISPLAYS.md. ABI60 adds copied RGBA scene pixels with native alpha composition; same1..256 extent and2MiB aggregate cap.

Explicit checked PNG stream loading/saving only, no public handler registration or general image format support. ABI60 adds copied RGBA scene pixels with native alpha composition; same1..256 extent and2MiB aggregate cap.
